- 2026-10-18 — B-101, Widevine on Netflix, investigated (Claude Code, M1 Max laptop; a side session of the backlog session, outside the phase order; build 14 compiling meanwhile, no Chromium build started). Findings in `research/notes/widevine.md`.
  - **Cause:** the CDM installs and registers (4.10.3112.0 in `RoomCore/WidevineCdm` since 2026-10-11) but never loads on the signed build. The running Room's log holds the one load attempt, at the user's Netflix visit: `dlopen` refused, "mapping process and mapped file (non-platform) have different Team IDs". The CDM process runs from `Room Helper (Aperitif).app`, hardened runtime, no `disable-library-validation`; Google's dylib is team EQHXZ8M8AV, Room N56PMJ99UT. The 2026-10-03 verification ran on the ad-hoc Debug host, where library validation does not apply, and release builds have no `-RoomSpike`.
  - **Measured** on clones of the Debug host signed by `roomcore-sign.sh --no-notarize` (31 s), each probed with `roomcore-features.sh widevine` from a profile seeded with the installed CDM (0 external hosts in the NetLogs): as shipped NotSupportedError "CreateCdmFunc not available." (4.9 s); `disable-library-validation` on the base helper only, the same failure; on the Aperitif helper, MediaKeys created (3.7 s); on both, created (3.1 s). Every run ended through the teardown watchdog (exit 2) at load average 580–690, with or without the fix.
  - **Other browsers** on this Mac (`codesign -d`): Arc, Dia, Brave, Vivaldi, Opera and Comet give their base/Aperitif helper `disable-library-validation` and ship a framework VMP `.sig`; Chrome needs neither exception (same team as the CDM); Edge re-signs a bundled CDM. Room has neither.
  - **VMP:** Chromium compiles CDM host verification only for Chrome-branded builds (`enable_cdm_host_verification`, `AddCdmHostFilePaths` is `NOTIMPLEMENTED` unbranded) and the public checkout has no `signature_generator.py`. Netflix (and most premium services) refuse licences to unverified macOS clients; castlabs' free EVS signs only their Electron builds, so a certificate means Google's agreement or castlabs 3PL.
  - **Docs:** B-101 rewritten with the cause and a two-step fix (step 1: `Helper-CDM.entitlements` on the base and Aperitif helpers in `sign-and-notarize.sh`, no build, awaiting the user's go-ahead; step 2: VMP with a certificate, a GN arg, a `chromium_src` shadow, a rebuild and a `.sig` signing step); PLAN §4 Widevine amended (the decision's "verify empirically" ran on the wrong build), the Entitlements row carries the proposal, §7 #9 updated.
  - **By-hand for the user** (`widevine.md` §4): Netflix's error code on build 13 and on the first DMG with step 1; Spotify Web and the Shaka Widevine demo on that DMG; `chrome://components`'s Widevine row.
  - No app or package code changed, so the CI spec was not rerun; not pushed.
- 2026-10-18 — B-105 researched: a self-hosted crash collector (Claude Code, M1 Max laptop; a side session of the backlog work, outside the phase order; research only, no code). Compared GlitchTip, Bugsink, self-hosted Sentry and a bare collector with `minidump-stackwalk`/`dump_syms` on minidump ingestion, symbols, Sentry SDK compatibility, footprint, licence and maintenance; `research/notes/crash-reporting.md` has the table, the sources and the wiring.
  - **Recommendation:** a small collector of our own (Crashpad's multipart POST at the Sentry minidump URL shape, derived from `ROOM_SENTRY_DSN`), `minidump-stackwalk` v0.27.0 with Breakpad symbols from `dump_syms` uploaded per release, a triage script grouping by crashing frame. GlitchTip 6.2.6 (MIT, Postgres, 512 MB) shows one frame per thread from a minidump until its issue 511 ships; Bugsink 2.6.1 (PolyForm Shield, SQLite) has minidumps experimental and broken (issue 530, fixed on `main` 2026-10-05) and no dSYM support (issue 20); self-hosted Sentry needs 16 GB RAM + swap. GlitchTip is the upgrade path (same URL shape).
  - **Evidence:** `cargo install minidump-stackwalk` built in about three minutes; on a real Room dump (1.05 MB, `Crashpad/pending`) it walked the crashing thread to 44 frames in 0.34 s with no symbols. Not done: a symbolicated run (build 14's `out/release` was mid-build and its `RoomCore.dSYM` empty).
  - **Facts for 6.3:** Chromium's `GetUploadUrl()` is empty and `GetCollectStatsConsent()` false for a non-Chrome-branded build, so Room uploads nothing today; enabling it is one `//room` hook (URL from the app's DSN, consent from `Settings.crashReports`, which is off by default). Sentry Cocoa (`SentryCrashReporter`) is redundant next to Crashpad (the app process calls `ChromeMain`) and a second crash handler; recommended dropped in 6.3. Dumps stay local when uploads are off.
  - **Applied:** B-105's row (recommendation, open question for the user), PLAN §4 Distribution and §7 #13. ROADMAP 6.3 is unchanged. The `.sym` size for `RoomCore` at `symbol_level = 1` and the symbolicated run are 6.3's first measurements.

(2) A row's states are in its label, not its value: the outline's cell element passes a cell view's label through and drops its value (read back from the tree). (3) Full Keyboard Access is simulated in-process (`KeyboardAccess.allControlsOverride`): AppKit reads the system setting itself (an argument-domain `AppleKeyboardUIMode` and a swizzled getter both left `NSButton` unchanged), and the setting is the user's (Phase 5.3 step 2); AppKit's own buttons in Settings follow the real setting only. (4) The audit's SwiftUI trees need `AXEnhancedUserInterface` set on the app before its hosting views exist, so the `a11y` and `keyboard` spikes set it at launch (DEBUG); the window test sets it before building its window. (5) The window test lives in a new `AccessibilityAuditTests` target in Palette's package (the highest package; it adds UIKitCore, Library and Chrome as test dependencies), because App has no test target; Peek, split panes and Settings (App's) are audited by the spike only. (6) An intermediate run of the keyboard spike pressed Return on the palette's "Search Google" top hit, which loaded a Google search for "Focus 3" in that run's throwaway state: a request the check did not need; the spike now moves with ↓ until VoiceOver hears the tab's row and presses Esc otherwise. (7) No computer use (`app.room.desktop.dev` not granted): every check here ran by spike hooks; the real setting and VoiceOver are the user's steps.

- 2026-10-22 — Phase 5.4, B-152 (Claude Code, Mac mini, worktree `/Volumes/JL4/room-wt/b152` on `fix-b152` from `e13fd73`; the apps over the staged engines, `ROOM_PACKAGE_STAGED=1`; no engine change). No `git pull` (a worktree; the planner merges). Commits `05f0638` (the fix and its unit test), `7d9a200` (the spike's key probe) and this entry.
  - **Found:** the host, not the harness. Since B-37 (`03fc57c`, 2026-10-21) the Tab menu's Focus Pane 1–4 are drawn as ⌃⇧digit (key "1"–"4" with a Shift mask) and AppKit matches such an item to ⌃digit without Shift; the Tab menu comes before Spaces and the item is disabled outside a split, so ⌃1–⌃4 did nothing. ⌃5 has no Focus Pane item, hence the spike's one switch per run. A user saw it (the spike's key probe: ⌃2, ⌃3, ⌃1 … left space 1 shown). Bisect not needed: the unit tier reproduced it in one test.
  - **Fix:** `KeyMatchingMenu` (UIKitCore), the bar's menus and submenus: while AppKit searches a menu, a drawn item (visible, Shift in its mask, a shifted US digit or punctuation key) whose modifiers without Shift are the event's has no key equivalent; its hidden alias still matches the shifted key. Unit test "B-152" presses ⌃2 (Space 2) and ⌃⇧2 (Focus Pane 2) through the bar; it failed before the fix.
  - **Proof:** the `spaces` spike 0 of 60 missed on dev (155) and on the release framework (156) (spike-results "B-152"); UIKitCore 63 / 63; the CI spec run locally (`xcodegen --spec xcodegen/ci.yml`, `CI=1` `build test`, `build/ci-b152`): 740 tests, 0 failed, 19 skipped; the normal project regenerated; `swift build -c release` in UIKitCore and the Release app compile. Computer use not used (the key path is `NSApp.sendEvent`, in-process). Not pushed (the planner merges).

  - **Found:** the `NSStatusBarWindow` is OmniboxEverywhere's "Search with Room" status item (the button's tooltip, `IDS_OMNIBOX_EVERYWHERE_STATUS_ICON_TOOLTIP`), not Glic's; 156's field-trial testing config enables `OmniboxEverywhere` on mac (155's has no entry).   - **Proof:** `-RoomSpikeStatusBarWatch YES` (DEBUG, any spike: the status-bar windows at start and end, the key after which one appears, its button's tooltip, image and menu) on the media spike: `80b2b9e` one window from start to end; `--disable-features=OmniboxEverywhere` on `80b2b9e` none; `b5f394a` none, `videopip_windows` without it, 0 dumps (spike-results "the menu-bar status item on 156"). Deviation (1): the CG window list could not show the window (no entry for any owner), so the repro and the after check read `NSApp.windows`. The CI spec not run: no push from a worktree, and the only app change is a `#if DEBUG` spike file. Computer use not used.
